Method: 构建了包含4,196个精心策划的交互对的BLADE数据集；采用LoRA适配器结合4-bit NormalFloat (NF4)量化框架，对DeepSeek-8B和LLaMA-3.2-3B等架构进行参数高效微调。

Result: 微调后的模型在结构保真度和敬语对齐方面取得了实质性改进，为低资源多语言文本生成中的语用差异弥合提供了严格的基准。

Conclusion: BLADE数据集和微调框架有效提升了模型在孟加拉语等低资源场景下的文化对齐能力，弥合了语用鸿沟。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multilingual Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly enhanced cross-lingual conversational capabilities, yet modeling culturally nuanced and context-dependent communication remains a critical bottleneck. Specifically, existing state-of-the-art models exhibit a severe pragmatic gap when handling structural variations, regional idioms, and honorific consistencies in low-resource contexts like Bangla. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel, culturally aligned instruction-tuning dataset for \textbf{BangLa Application and DialoguE generation - BLADE} and benchmarking framework comprising $4,196$ meticulously curated interaction pairs. We leverage this resource to systematically fine-tune and evaluate leading open-weight architectures, including DeepSeek-8B and LLaMA-3.2-3B, utilizing parameter-efficient fine-tuning via LoRA adapters in a 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) quantization framework. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our dataset yield substantial improvements in structural fidelity and honorific alignment, providing a rigorous benchmark for bridging pragmatic disparities in low-resource multilingual text generation. Code and dataset: https://github.com/ashuvo25/Bangla_Application_LLM/tree/main

</details>

*Darya Shlyk,Stefano Montanelli,Lawrence Hunter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指令微调的开源生成模型作为重排序器的方法，应用于生物医学实体链接（BEL）流程的重排序阶段。该方法采用集合式指令微调公式，实现了快速准确的候选选择，在多个BEL基准上提升了3%-24%的链接准确率，同时降低了推理时间，并集成到了端到端系统BeLink中。


<details>

Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的生物医学实体链接方法在计算效率低和实际部署困难的问题，需要一种更高效的解决方案。

Method: 在BEL流程的重排序阶段使用指令微调的开源生成模型，采用集合式（set-wise）指令微调公式进行快速准确的候选选择。

Result: 在多个BEL基准上，链接准确率提升3%-24%，同时推理时间相比现有最优方法有所降低。

Conclusion: 指令微调的开源生成模型作为重排序器是BEL任务的有效且实用的解决方案，集成的BeLink系统适用于实际应用场景。

Abstract: Despite recent progress, Biomedical Entity Linking (BEL) with large language models (LLMs) remains computationally inefficient and challenging to deploy in practical settings. In this work, we demonstrate that instruction-tuning of open-source generative models can offer an effective solution when applied at the re-ranking stage of the BEL pipeline. We propose a set-wise instruction-tuning formulation that enables fast and accurate candidate selection. Our method demonstrates strong performance on multiple BEL benchmarks, yielding significant improvements in linking accuracy (3%-24%) while reducing inference time compared to the state-of-the-art. We integrate our generative re-ranker into BeLink, a modular, end-to-end system designed for practical real-world BEL applications.

</details>


### [39] [Scene Abstraction for Lexical Semantics: Structured Representations of Situated Meaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22542)
*Yejin Cho,Katrin Erk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Scene Abstraction框架，通过大语言模型的少样本提示，从上下文中提取结构化场景表示（包含情境场景和表达轮廓），并构建了COCA-Scenes数据集。实验表明该方法在场景识别（82.4%准确率）和与人类解释一致性（86.4%偏好）上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 咖啡和茶虽有许多共同属性，却激发截然不同的情境、氛围和情感联想。这种词汇意义的情境维度是真实且系统的，但在当前大多数计算词汇表征中仍被隐含处理，缺乏结构化表示。

Method: 提出Scene Abstraction框架，将每个场景分解为情境场景（事件、实体、环境）和表达轮廓（参与事件、可推广属性、唤起情感），通过大语言模型的少样本提示实现操作化。

Result: （1）构建了包含520个用法实例（26个关键词）的COCA-Scenes数据集；（2）场景识别任务达到82.4%准确率，比纯文本嵌入提升11.8个百分点；（3）场景轮廓在三个语义维度上比ATOMIC替代方案更符合人类对上下文中词汇的解释（86.4%偏好）。

Conclusion: 词汇意义的情境维度可以通过结构化场景表示来捕捉，该方法在识别场景一致性和贴近人类解释方面均优于现有方法，为词汇语义的深层计算建模提供了新方向。

Abstract: Coffee and tea share many properties, yet they evoke strikingly different situations, atmospheres, and affective associations. These situated dimensions of word meaning are real and systematic, but they remain implicit in most computational representations of lexical meaning. We propose Scene Abstraction, a framework for constructing structured representations of the interpretive scenes that words participate in across usage contexts. Each scene consists of a Contextual Scene (Events, Entities, Setting) and an expression-centered Expression Profile (Engaged events, Generalizable properties, Evoked emotions), operationalized through few-shot prompting of a large language model. Our contributions are three-fold: (1) a structured representation framework for situated lexical meaning; (2) COCA-Scenes, a dataset of 520 usage instances across 26 keywords for distinct scene identification; and (3) empirical evidence from two experiments suggesting that scenes are reliably identifiable across human observers (82.4% accuracy, +11.8 pp over text-only embeddings) and that our scene profiles more closely align with human interpretation of words in context than ATOMIC-based alternatives (86.4% preference across three semantic dimensions).

</details>


### [40] [One prompt is not enough: Instruction Sensitivity Undermines Embedding Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22544)
*Yevhen Kostiuk,Kenneth Enevoldsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，当前基于指令的嵌入模型评估仅使用单条提示词，忽略了模型对提示词措辞的敏感性。通过对6个模型、11个数据集、990次评估，发现默认提示词可能系统性地低估或高估性能，且通过选择有利提示词，任何模型均可升至排行榜第一，说明单提示评估不足以评估指令微调嵌入模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的指令嵌入模型评估仅使用单条提示词，忽略了模型对提示词措辞的敏感性，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 选取6种嵌入模型、11个数据集，每个数据集设计15个任务特定提示词，共计990次评估，进行实证研究。

Result: 默认提示词可能系统性地低估或高估模型性能；通过选择有利提示词，任意模型均可被推至排行榜第一名；排行榜排名对提示词选择不鲁棒。

Conclusion: 单提示评估不足以评估指令微调嵌入模型，基准测试应纳入提示鲁棒性，要么使用多提示评估，要么在点估计之外报告敏感性指标。

Abstract: Instruction embedding models have become common among state-of-the-art models, however are evaluated using a single prompt per task. The single-point evaluation ignores a main problem of the instruction-based approach namely: sensitivity to the phrasing of the instruction. We present an empirical study of prompt sensitivity across 6 embedding models, 11 datasets, and 15 task-specific prompts per dataset, a total of 990. We show that reported scores misrepresent the distribution of scores over plausible prompts. The default prompt can both systematically understate or overstate performance. Furthermore, we show that the leaderboard ranking is not robust to prompt selection: by choosing prompts favorably, any model in our study can be promoted to first place. Our findings suggest that single-prompt evaluation is insufficient for instruction-tuned embedding models and that benchmarks should incorporate prompt robustness, either by evaluating over multiple prompts or by reporting sensitivity alongside point estimates.

</details>


### [41] [SynAE: A Framework for Measuring the Quality of Synthetic Data for Tool-Calling Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22564)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Aadyaa Maddi,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynAE是一个评估框架，用于衡量多轮工具调用智能体的合成基准测试在多大程度上能够复制和增强真实数据轨迹的特征，从任务指令、工具调用、最终输出和下游评估四个维度评估合成数据的有效性、保真度和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的内部数据集通常存在不足或无法直接用于测试（如包含敏感数据、过于稀疏），因此实践中越来越多地使用合成数据替代或增强真实数据集进行评估。现有方法缺乏量化合成数据与真实数据之间关系的系统性框架。

Method: 提出SynAE框架，从四个类别评估合成数据质量：(i) 任务指令与中间响应，(ii) 工具调用，(iii) 最终输出，(iv) 下游评估。通过实际和可控的生成方案，在多个智能体基准上测试常见的合成数据失败模式。

Result: SynAE能够检测合成数据在有效性、保真度和多样性上的细粒度变化，实验表明没有任何单一指标足以完整刻画合成数据质量，需要多轴评估。

Conclusion: 合成数据评估需要多维度、多指标的综合评估框架，单一指标不能充分评估合成数据质量，SynAE为多轮工具调用智能体的合成数据评估提供了系统化方案。

Abstract: Today, tool-calling agents are commonly evaluated or tested on static datasets of execution traces, including input commands, agent responses, and associated tool calls. However, internal production datasets are often insufficient or unusable for testing; for example, they may contain sensitive or proprietary data, or they may be too sparse to support comprehensive testing (especially pre-deployment). In these settings, practitioners are increasingly replacing or augmenting real datasets with synthetic ones for evaluation purposes. A key challenge is quantifying the relation between these synthetic datasets and the real data. We introduce SynAE, an evaluation framework for assessing how well synthetic benchmarks for multi-turn, tool-calling agents replicate and augment the characteristics of real data trajectories. SynAE assesses the validity, fidelity, and diversity of synthetic data across four metric categories: (i) task instructions and intermediate responses, (ii) tool calls, (iii) final outputs, and (iv) downstream evaluation. We evaluate SynAE using recent agent benchmarks and test common synthetic data failure modes via realistic and controlled generation schemes. SynAE detects fine-grained variations in data validity, fidelity and diversity, and shows that no single metric is sufficient to fully characterize synthetic data quality, motivating a multi-axis evaluation of synthetic data for agent testing. A demo of SynAE is available at https://synae-2026-synae-demo.static.hf.space/index.html, with code at https://github.com/wsqwsq/SynAE.

</details>


### [42] [LANG: Reinforcement Learning for Multilingual Reasoning with Language-Adaptive Hint Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22567)
*Yuchun Fan,Bei Li,Peiguang Li,Yilin Wang,Yongyu Mu,Jian Yang,Xin Chen,Rongxiang Weng,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LANG是一个利用语言条件提示来引导非英语推理任务探索的框架，通过渐进式衰减调度和语言自适应切换机制，在不损害语言一致性的前提下提升了多语言推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在多语言推理场景中面临根本性权衡：优先保证输入语言一致性会严重损害推理质量，而优先保证推理质量则会导致语言向英语漂移，尚未充分解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出LANG框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 渐进式衰减调度，逐步撤销语言条件提示的支撑；(2) 语言自适应切换，根据特定语言难度定制学习周期，以平衡推理性能与语言一致性。

Result: 在具有挑战性的多语言数学基准测试中，LANG显著提升了推理性能，同时未损害语言一致性；此外，该框架能泛化至数学之外的领域，促进模型各层之间更一致的语言对齐。

Conclusion: LANG有效解决了多语言推理中语言一致性与推理质量之间的权衡问题，证明通过精心设计的语言引导机制可以在不牺牲语言保真度的前提下提升非英语推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven effective for enhancing multi-step reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet its benefits have not fully translated to multilingual contexts. Existing methods struggle with a fundamental trade-off: prioritizing input-language consistency severely hampers reasoning quality, while prioritizing reasoning often leads to unintended language drift toward English. We address this challenge with LANG, a novel framework that leverages language-conditioned hints to guide exploration in non-English reasoning tasks. Our method incorporates two key mechanisms to prevent dependency on these hints: a progressive decay schedule that gradually withdraws scaffolding, and a language-adaptive switch that tailors learning horizons to specific language difficulties. Empirical results on challenging multilingual mathematical benchmarks reveal that LANG substantially enhances reasoning performance without compromising language consistency. Moreover, we show that our framework generalizes beyond mathematics, fostering more consistent language alignment across model layers

</details>


### [43] [Beyond Temperature: Hyperfitting as a Late-Stage Geometric Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22579)
*Meimingwei Li,Yuanhao Ding,Esteban Garces Arias,Christian Heumann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现"超拟合"（Hyperfitting）现象——微调LLM至接近零训练损失可提升生成质量并减少重复——其机制与温度缩放和静态词汇重加权根本不同，归因于最终Transformer模块中特征空间的几何扩展（维度增加约80.8），并提出了仅更新最后5层的Late-Stage LoRA策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示"超拟合"（Hyperfitting）现象的内在机制，澄清其与简单的分布锐化（温度缩放）或静态词汇重加权的区别。

Method: 通过熵匹配对照实验排除温度缩放解释；通过消融研究排除静态词汇重加权假设；通过逐层分析定位超拟合效应来源；提出Late-Stage LoRA（仅更新最后5层）。

Result: 超拟合与温度缩放根本不同且无法通过静态词汇重加权解释；其机制在于最终Transformer模块的"Terminal Expansion"——特征空间几何维度显著扩展（约增加80.8），促进深度尾部token的提升。

Conclusion: 超拟合本质上是一种动态、上下文相关的秩重排序机制，源于最终层的特征空间几何扩展；Late-Stage LoRA可以极少的参数更新实现稳健生成。

Abstract: Recent work has identified a counterintuitive phenomenon termed "Hyperfitting", where fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) to near-zero training loss on small datasets surprisingly enhances open-ended generation quality and mitigates repetition in greedy decoding. While effective, the underlying mechanism remains poorly understood, with the extremely low-entropy output distributions suggesting a potential equivalence to simple temperature scaling. In this work, we demonstrate that this phenomenon is fundamentally distinct from distribution sharpening; entropy-matched control experiments reveal that temperature scaling fails to replicate the diversity gains of hyperfitting. Furthermore, we falsify the hypothesis of static vocabulary reweighting, showing through ablation studies that hyperfitting relies on a dynamic, context-dependent rank reordering mechanism. Layer-wise analysis localizes this effect to a "Terminal Expansion" in the final transformer block, where a substantial geometric expansion of the feature space (Delta Dim approx +80.8) facilitates the promotion of deep-tail tokens. Additionally, we introduce Late-Stage LoRA, a targeted fine-tuning strategy that updates only the final 5 layers, yielding robust generation with minimal parameter updates

</details>


### [44] [Agentic CLEAR: Automating Multi-Level Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22608)
*Asaf Yehudai,Lilach Eden,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agentic CLEAR 是一个自动、动态、易用的智能体评估框架，能在系统、轨迹和节点三个粒度上生成文本洞察，支持无缝集成，实验结果与人工标注错误高度对齐并能预测任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估工具能力有限，要么只关注可观测性和基础评估，要么依赖静态、手工构建的错误分类体系，无法适应新领域。亟需一种自动、动态、易用的评估框架。

Method: 提出Agentic CLEAR框架，在可观测层之上运行，支持系统级、轨迹级和节点级三个粒度的文本洞察输出，配备直观UI，实现无缝集成。

Result: 在四个基准测试、七种智能体设置和数万次大模型调用上验证，Agentic CLEAR生成高质量、数据驱动的洞察反馈，与人工标注错误高度对齐，并能预测任务成功率。

Conclusion: Agentic CLEAR有效弥补了现有智能体评估工具的不足，提供自动、动态、多粒度的评估能力，具有良好的领域适应性。

Abstract: Agentic systems are becoming more capable: agents define strategies, take actions, and interact with different environments. This autonomy poses serious challenges for overseeing and assessing agent behavior. Most current tools are limited, focusing on observability with basic evaluation capabilities or imposing static, hand-crafted error taxonomies that cannot adapt to new domains. To address this gap, we present Agentic CLEAR, an automatic, dynamic, and easy-to-use evaluation framework. It produces textual insights into the agent behavior on three levels of granularity: system, trace, and node. Agentic CLEAR operates above the observability layer, enabling seamless integration and featuring an intuitive UI that makes agent evaluation highly accessible. In our experiments on four benchmarks, seven agentic settings, and tens of thousands of LLM calls, we show that Agentic CLEAR produces high-quality, data-driven, insightful feedback. Our analysis shows strong alignment with human-annotated errors and the ability to predict task success rate.

</details>


### [45] [Chinese sensorimotor and embodiment norms for 3,000 lexicalized concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22616)
*Jing Chen,Gábor Parti,Yin Zhong,Chu-Ren Huang,Marco Marelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含3,000个汉语词汇的11维感觉运动规范数据库，验证了感知强度（PSE）等指标在词汇决策中的预测效度，并发现纯语言表征可部分恢复感觉运动信息（平均Spearman r=.62），支持语言分布中蕴含具身概念结构的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念知识如何植根于身体体验、机器能否在缺乏直接感觉运动经验的情况下获取此类知识，是认知科学与具身人工智能的核心问题；而大规模规范资源在非印欧语系中严重匮乏。

Method: 收集378名普通话母语者对3,000个词汇的11维感觉运动评分和一维具身性评分；通过交叉验证检验信度与效度；在词汇决策任务中比较PSE-Sensorimotor、Minkowski-3等复合变量的预测能力；使用简单回归模型从语言表征恢复感觉运动评分，并采用表征相似性分析（RSA）评估空间几何结构的可恢复性。

Result: 评分具有高信度和强交叉效度；PSE-Sensorimotor和Minkowski-3是词汇决策绩效的最强预测因子；感觉运动评分可从语言表征部分恢复（平均r=.62），视觉和听觉维度恢复度高于化学感知维度；感觉运动空间的几何结构也可部分恢复（r=.540）。

Conclusion: 大规模汉语感觉运动数据库为跨语言具身认知研究提供了重要资源；纯语言分布编码了具身概念结构的某些方面，但不同感觉模态的编码程度存在差异。

Abstract: Understanding how conceptual knowledge is grounded in bodily experience, and to what extent machine systems can acquire such knowledge without direct sensorimotor experience, are central questions in both cognitive science and embodied artificial intelligence research. Large-scale normative resources are essential for investigating these questions empirically, yet such resources remain sparse for non-Indo-European languages. We present a novel normative database for 3,000 lexicalized concepts in Mandarin Chinese, comprising 11-dimensional sensorimotor ratings and unidimensional embodiment ratings collected from 378 native Mandarin speakers. The ratings demonstrate high reliability and strong cross-norm validity with existing Chinese resources, each of which covers fewer words and a subset of the 11 sensorimotor dimensions. In a validation study, we tested new variables derived from a theoretically motivated metric, Perceptual Strength of Embodiment (PSE) (Huang et al., 2025), together with seven common composite variables, on lexical decision tasks. The results suggest that PSE-Sensorimotor and Minkowski-3 are the strongest composite predictors of lexical decision performance, capturing the facilitatory effects of sensorimotor information on lexical processing. A further exploratory study showed that sensorimotor ratings are substantially recoverable from purely linguistic representations using simple regression models (mean Spearman r = .62 across dimensions), though recovery varied markedly: visual and auditory dimensions yielded higher correspondence than chemosensory ones. Representational similarity analysis further showed that the relational geometry of the sensorimotor space is also partially recoverable (r = .540), consistent with the view that distributional language use encodes aspects of embodied conceptual structure.

</details>


### [46] [More Context, Larger Models, or Moral Knowledge? A Systematic Study of Schwartz Value Detection in Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22641)
*Víctor Yeste,Paolo Rosso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了上下文长度、检索增强知识、模型规模对政治价值观检测的影响，发现更多上下文并不总是更好，检索知识在匹配比较中更稳定有效，模型规模增大不保证性能提升，且不同价值观对上下文和知识的敏感度存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测施瓦茨价值观时，文本中的隐式线索常依赖于周围论证及相邻价值观间的细微区分，这使得句子级别的价值观检测具有挑战性，现有研究对何时需要上下文和显性道德知识缺乏系统理解。

Method: 采用ValuesML/Touché ValueEval格式，比较了句子/窗口/全文三种输入方式、无检索与基于策展道德知识库的检索增强设置、DeBERTa-v3-base/large监督编码器以及12B-123B参数的零样本大语言模型，并测试了早期融合、晚期融合和交叉注意力三种RAG变体。

Result: 全文上下文使监督DeBERTa编码器提升3.8-4.8个Macro-F1点，但对零样本LLM无一致帮助；检索知识在匹配比较中对各模型族和上下文条件均有改善；从DeBERTa-base到large以及12B到更大LLM的规模扩展不保证性能提升；早期融合优于晚期融合和交叉注意力RAG变体；上下文和检索对社会情境性或概念易混淆的价值观帮助最大。

Conclusion: 价值观敏感的NLP应当联合评估上下文、知识和模型家族，而非将更长输入或更大模型视为普遍改进手段。

Abstract: Detecting Schwartz values in political text is difficult because implicit cues often depend on surrounding arguments and fine-grained distinctions between neighboring values. We study when context and explicit moral knowledge help sentence-level value detection. Using the ValuesML/Touch{é} ValueEval format, we compare sentence, window, and full-document inputs; no-RAG and retrieval-augmented settings with a curated moral knowledge base; supervised DeBERTa-v3-base/large encoders; and zero-shot LLMs from 12B to 123B parameters. The results show that more context is not uniformly better: full-document context improves supervised DeBERTa encoders by 3.8--4.8 macro-F1 points over sentence-only input, but does not consistently help zero-shot LLMs. Retrieved moral knowledge is more consistently useful in matched comparisons, improving each tested model family and context condition under early fusion. However, scaling from DeBERTa-v3-base to large and from 12B to larger LLMs does not guarantee gains, and simple early fusion outperforms the tested late-fusion and cross-attention RAG variants for encoders. Per-value analyses show that context and retrieval help most for socially situated or conceptually confusable values. These findings suggest that value-sensitive NLP should evaluate context, knowledge, and model family jointly rather than treating longer inputs or larger models as universal improvements.

</details>

*Shanshan Wang,Fengying Ye,Hanjia Lyu,Caiwen Gou,Junchao Wu,Jingming Yao,Chengzhong Xu,Jiebo Luo,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估并增强了LLM作为现代汉语诗歌检测器的性能，提出了图像-语义引导的诗歌检测方法，通过引入反映诗歌内容的图像来辅助判断。实验表明，该方法优于基于纯文本的基线检测器，甚至超越最佳传统检测器RoBERTa，Gemini检测器达到85.65%的Macro-F1分数，达到SOTA水平。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明LLM不能有效用作检测器，但这些研究未涉及现代汉语诗歌领域，且尚无相关研究探索LLM在现代汉语诗歌检测上的表现。因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出图像-语义引导的诗歌检测方法，创新性地引入反映诗歌内容的图像，通过示例驱动的方式有效整合图像中的意义、意象和情感等信息，与诗歌文本形成互补判断。

Conclusion: 本文提出的图像-语义引导方法能有效提升LLM在现代汉语诗歌检测任务上的性能，多个LLM检测器的性能提升证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Previous detection studies have shown that LLMs cannot be effectively used as detectors, but these studies have not addressed modern Chinese poetry. Moreover, no relevant research has explored the performance of LLMs in detecting modern Chinese poetry. This paper evaluates and enhances the performance of LLMs as detectors for modern Chinese poetry, and proposes an image-semantic guided poetry detection method. Compared with traditional detection approaches, our method innovatively incorporates images that reflect the content of the poetry. Through example-driven approaches, our method effectively integrates information such as meaning, imagery, and feeling from the image, then forms a complementary judgment with the poem text. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM detectors based on our method outperform baseline detectors based on plain text, and even surpass the best-performing traditional detector, RoBERTa. The Gemini detector using our method achieves a Macro-F1 score of 85.65%, reaching the state-of-the-art level. The performance improvements of different LLM detectors on multiple LLMs-generated data prove the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [48] [Moral Semantics Survive Machine Translation: Cross-Lingual Evidence from Moral Foundations Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22660)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究以波兰语为测试案例，通过四种验证方法证明，尽管机器翻译在处理俚语、粗俗语和文化表达方面存在不足，但LLM翻译仍能有效保留道德语义信息，可用于跨语言道德价值观分类，平均余弦相似度0.86，AUC差距仅0.01-0.02。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道德语言具有微妙性和文化变异性，而现有道德价值观自动分类依赖几乎仅限于英语的标注语料库，导致其他语言（如波兰语）在该领域资源极度匮乏。作者旨在探索基于LLM的翻译能否弥合这一跨语言鸿沟。

Method: 使用约5万条涵盖多种话题的波兰语道德标注社交媒体帖子，构建四阶段验证流程：LaBSE跨语言嵌入相似度、Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)、LLM-as-judge评估以及深度学习分类器一致性测试。

Result: 直接翻译在平均余弦相似度达到0.86，各道德基础维度的AUC差距仅为0.01-0.02；在语言模型微调后差距进一步缩小。但在处理俚语、粗俗语和文化负载表达时仍存在不足。

Conclusion: 机器翻译是面向当前道德价值观资源不足语言的一种实用且经济有效的路径，以波兰语作为代表性斯拉夫语验证，预期可推广至相关语言。

Abstract: Moral language is subtle and culturally variable, making it difficult to translate faithfully across languages. Idiomatic expressions, slang, and cultural references introduce hard-to-avoid translation artifacts. Yet automated moral values classification depends on language-specific annotated corpora that exist almost exclusively in English. We investigate whether LLM-based translation can bridge this gap, taking Polish as a test case. Using $\sim$50k morally-annotated social media posts from a diverse range of topics, we apply a principled four-method validation pipeline: LaBSE cross-lingual embedding similarity, Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), LLM-as-judge evaluation, and deep learning classifier parity tests. We show that despite shortcomings in handling slang, vulgarity, and culturally-loaded expressions, direct translation preserves subtle moral cues well enough to be harvested by cross-lingual machine learning -- with mean cosine similarity of 0.86 and AUC gaps of 0.01--0.02 across all foundations closing further under fine-tuning of language models. These results demonstrate that machine translation is a practical and cost-effective path to moral values research in languages currently under-resourced in this domain. We demonstrate this for Polish as a representative Slavic language, with expected generalisation to related languages.

</details>


### [49] [Self-Policy Distillation via Capability-Selective Subspace Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22675)
*Guangya Hao,Yitong Shang,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao,Hanxue Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Policy Distillation (SPD)，一种无需外部信号、可泛化且能力可选择的自我蒸馏方法。SPD通过从模型自身梯度中提取低秩能力子空间，在自生成过程中投影KV激活，并在原始输出上微调。在代码生成、数学推理和多选题问答任务上，SPD较现有方法提升最高13%，较预训练基线提升最高16%，并在域外泛化场景下提升15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我蒸馏方法要么依赖外部信号（正确性过滤、执行反馈、奖励搜索等），对前沿模型成本高昂且不可用；要么完全不筛选原始输出，导致领域特定且难以泛化。两类方法共同的问题是自生成输出将任务相关能力与风格模式、格式伪影、模型特定错误等纠缠在一起，稀释了待提升能力的信号。

Method: SPD包含三步：1) 从模型自身在正确性定义词元上的梯度中提取低秩能力子空间；2) 在自生成过程中将KV激活投影到该子空间；3) 对生成的原始输出使用标准下一个词元预测损失进行微调。整个过程无需任何外部信号。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和多选题问答任务上，SPD较现有无外部信号的自我蒸馏方法提升最高13%，较预训练基线提升最高16%。在域外泛化设置下，SPD表现提升15%，展示了优越的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SPD通过低秩能力子空间的提取与投影，实现了无需外部信号、可泛化且能力可选择的自我蒸馏，有效解耦了目标任务能力与干扰信号，显著提升了LLM自我蒸馏的效果和泛化性。

Abstract: Self-distillation bootstraps large language models (LLMs) by training on their own generations. However, existing methods either rely on external signals to curate self-generated outputs (e.g., correctness filtering, execution feedback, and reward search), which are costly and unavailable for the best-performing frontier models, or skip curation entirely and train on all raw outputs, an approach that is often domain-specific and hard to generalize. Both also share a deeper weakness that self-generated outputs entangle task-relevant capability with others, such as stylistic patterns, formatting artifacts, and model-specific errors, diluting the signal for the specific capability one aims to improve. In this paper, we propose Self-Policy Distillation (SPD), which achieves generalizable, capability selective without any external signal. Specifically, SPD extracts a low-rank capability subspace from the model's own gradients on correctness-defining tokens, projects key-value (KV) activations into this subspace during self-generation, and fine-tunes on the resulting raw outputs with standard next-token prediction loss. Through extensive experiments across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and multiple-choice QA, we show that SPD achieves up to 13% improvement over state-of-the-art self-distillation methods without external signals and up to 16% improvement over pre-trained baselines. Notably, SPD demonstrates superior generalizability, achieving 15% better performance under out-of-domain generalization settings.

</details>


### [50] [Tokenization with Split Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22705)
*Craig W. Schmidt,Michael Krumdick,Adam Wiemerslage,Seth Ebner,Varshini Reddy,Yuval Pinter,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToaST是一种基于分裂树的子词分词方法，通过递归推理和整数规划优化词汇表，在英语文本上相比BPE、WordPiece和UnigramLM减少11%以上的token数量，并在1.5B参数语言模型训练中取得最优CORE分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子词分词方法（如BPE、WordPiece、UnigramLM）在压缩效率和token数量上存在改进空间，无法有效最大化有效上下文长度。

Method: 提出Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST)方法：利用预计算的字节n-gram频次将每个预分词贪心地构建为满二叉树；推理时递归遍历分裂树，输出每条路径上首个在词汇表中的节点；词汇表选择建模为整数规划（IP）问题，最小化所有分裂树的总token数，其LP松弛在实践中近似整数解，训练复杂度与分裂树数量呈二次关系。

Result: 在词汇量≥40,960时，ToaST相比BPE、WordPiece和UnigramLM减少11%以上的token数量；减少了对常见单字节token的使用频率，显著提升Renyi效率；在1.5B参数语言模型训练中，CORE分数超越基线2.6%-7.6%，在22个任务中的13个上取得最佳表现。

Conclusion: ToaST通过优化压缩效率实现了更少的token数量和更长的有效上下文长度，在大规模语言模型训练中表现出显著优越性，是一种有效的子词分词替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), a subword tokenization method that directly optimizes compression under a new recursive inference procedure. ToaST greedily splits each pretoken into a full binary tree using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of any vocabulary. Given a vocabulary, inference recursively descends each split tree and emits the first in-vocabulary node reached on each path. Vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program (IP) that minimizes the total token count over all split trees under this inference procedure. The Linear Programming (LP) relaxation is near-integral in practice, yielding provably near-optimal vocabularies, with training time empirically scaling quadratically in the number of split trees. On English text, ToaST reduces token counts by more than 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at vocabulary sizes of 40,960 and above, reducing the number of inference tokens for models using this tokenizer, thus extending the effective context length. ToaST also uses common single-byte tokens less frequently than these baselines, leading to a substantial improvement in Renyi efficiency. In experiments training 1.5B parameter language models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%--7.6%, with significance for two of three, and scoring best on 13 of 22 individual tasks.

</details>


### [51] [ChronoMedKG: A Temporally-Grounded Biomedical Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22734)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Farzaneh Firoozbakht,Lukas Galke Poech,Jan Baumbach,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChronoMedKG，一个包含460,497个时间感知三元组的生物医学知识图谱，覆盖13,431种疾病。通过多智能体流水线从文献中提取带有时间信息（发病窗口、进展阶段）的疾病关联，并引入ChronoTQA基准测试。实验表明，前沿LLM在时间推理任务上表现骤降（约30个百分点），而ChronoMedKG检索可恢复47-65%的长尾失败，显著优于HPOA-RAG的17-29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学知识图谱（如PrimeKG、Hetionet、iKraph）将疾病关联视为静态事实，缺乏时间信息（如症状在3岁和13岁时可能指向不同疾病），无法支持纵向临床推理与检索增强，限制了在时序临床推理中的应用。

Method: 采用疾病自主多智能体流水线：多个前沿LLM（大语言模型）独立从PubMed/PMC文献中提取知识，仅保留多模型共识、经可信度过滤和本体对齐的三元组，每个关联都包含时间组件（发病窗口、进展阶段）及PMID可追溯证据和多信号可信度评分。

Result: ChronoMedKG与Orphadata的一致性达92.7%，为6,250种疾病（含1,657种罕见病）添加了时间信息。在ChronoTQA基准上，LLM从静态问题到时间问题下降约30个百分点；ChronoMedKG检索可恢复47-65%的长尾失败，而HPOA-RAG仅恢复17-29%。

Conclusion: ChronoMedKG为临床检索增强系统提供了关键的时间维度，填补了现有知识图谱在时序推理能力上的空白，有效提升了LLM在时间敏感临床问题上的表现。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) treat disease associations as static facts, but temporal information is crucial for clinical reasoning, e.g., a symptom diagnostic of one disease at age 3 may imply a different disease at age 13. Existing KGs such as PrimeKG, Hetionet, and iKraph do not encode when a finding becomes clinically relevant over the course of a disease. This limits their usefulness for longitudinal clinical reasoning and retrieval augmentation.
  We introduce ChronoMedKG, a temporal biomedical knowledge graph that contains 460,497 evidence-linked triples (filtered from 13M raw extractions) covering 13,431 diseases. Each association is tied to temporal components like onset window or progression stage, which are backed by PMID-traceable evidence and a multi-signal credibility score. The graph is constructed through a disease-autonomous multi-agent pipeline in which multiple frontier LLMs independently extract knowledge from PubMed and PMC literature. Only those relations are kept that are supported by multi-model consensus, survive credibility filtering, as well as ontology alignment.
  ChronoMedKG scored 92.7% agreement against Orphadata and adds temporal grounding for 6,250 diseases absent from HPOA, Orphadata, and Phenopackets, including 1,657 Orphanet-coded rare diseases. We further introduce ChronoTQA, a benchmark of 3,341 questions across eight task types (six temporal plus two static controls), with a 12-question supplementary probe. Frontier LLMs lose roughly 30 points moving from static to temporal questions; ChronoMedKG retrieval rescues 47-65% of their long-tail failures, against 17-29% for HPOA-RAG. As such, ChronoMedKG provides a crucial temporal axis for retrieval-augmented clinical systems that was previously absent.

</details>


### [52] [Understanding Data Temporality Impact on Large Language Models Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22769)
*Pilchen Hippolyte,Fabre Romain,Signe Talla Franck,Perez Patrick,Grave Edouard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练数据顺序对大语言模型时间敏感知识获取的影响，发现按时间顺序训练的模型在保持通用语言能力的同时，比随机打乱训练的模型拥有更及时、更精确的时间感知知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常在打乱顺序的语料上训练，导致模型的知识冻结在训练时刻，其时间定位能力尚不明确。作者旨在探究预训练动态（尤其是数据顺序）对时间敏感事实知识获取的影响。

Method: 构建了一个包含7000+时间敏感问题的基准测试集及评估协议；在按时间排序的Common Crawl快照上预训练了6B参数模型，并与标准随机打乱预训练进行对比。

Result: 按时间顺序训练的模型在通用语言理解和常识知识上与随机打乱基线相当，但知识更及时、时间精度更高；随机打乱训练则在旧数据上表现更好，可能源于事实重复。

Conclusion: 按时间顺序的预训练能提升事实新鲜度，为LLM的持续学习研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically trained on shuffled corpora, yielding models whose knowledge is frozen at train time and whose temporal grounding remains poorly understood. In this work, we study the impact of pre-training dynamics on the acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, focusing specifically on data ordering. Our main contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a comprehensive benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and an evaluation protocol that enables analysis of whether models correctly associate facts with their corresponding time periods. Second, we pretrain 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots and compare them against standard shuffled pre-training. Our results show that sequentially trained models match shuffled baselines on general language understanding and common knowledge while consistently exhibiting more up-to-date and temporally precise knowledge. Temporally ordered pre-training yields improved factual freshness, while shuffled pre-training peaks on older data, possibly due to increased factual repetition. These findings, along with the release of our code at https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos , checkpoints, and datasets at https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos provide a foundation for future research on continual learning for LLMs.

</details>


### [53] [Reducing Political Manipulation with Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22771)
*Long Phan,Devin Kim,Alexander Pan,Alice Blair,Adam Khoja,Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在面对对立政治话题时存在系统性隐性政治偏见（7种技术类型），提出情感一致性（修辞对称性）和帮助一致性（参与度对称性）两个度量指标，并引入政治一致性训练（PCT，包含两种互补范式）的RL方法，在保持整体帮助性的同时显著降低隐性偏见，且能泛化到未见过基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM的显性政治偏见，但未系统刻画模型在处理对立政治话题时的不对称行为（隐性政治偏见），缺乏对此类偏见的量化指标和有效的缓解方法。

Method: 1. 从7类技术维度识别LLM中隐性政治偏见的操作方式；2. 提出两个度量指标：Sentiment Consistency（修辞与框架对称性）和Helpfulness Consistency（参与深度对称性）；3. 提出Political Consistency Training (PCT)，基于强化学习，包含Sentiment Consistency Training和Helpfulness Consistency Training两种互补范式。

Result: PCT在保持模型整体帮助性的前提下，显著降低了隐性政治偏见，并在未参与训练的基准数据集上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PCT能有效缓解LLM的隐性政治偏见且不影响模型原本的有用性，为构建更公平、中立的语言模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic political bias across a variety of sensitive contexts. We find that LLMs handle counterpart topics from opposing political sides asymmetrically. We refer to this phenomenon as covert political bias and identify 7 categories of techniques through which it operates. We propose two metrics for covert bias: Sentiment Consistency measures symmetry in rhetoric and framing across paired political prompts; Helpfulness Consistency measures symmetric depth and engagement. To reduce both types of covert bias, we introduce Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL training method with two complementary paradigms: Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training. We show that PCT preserves overall helpfulness, substantially reduces covert political bias, and generalizes to held-out benchmarks. We release our work at https://political-manipulation.ai

</details>


### [54] [Evaluating Commercial AI Chatbots as News Intermediaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22785)
*Mirac Suzgun,Emily Shen,Federico Bianchi,Alexander Spangher,Thomas Icard,Daniel E. Ho,Dan Jurafsky,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对6款主流AI聊天机器人（Gemini 3 Flash/Pro、Grok 4、Claude 4.5 Sonnet、GPT-5、GPT-4o mini）在14天内基于BBC同日报道的2100个事实问题进行了评估。最佳系统多项选择准确率超90%，但自由回答下降11-13%，所有模型在印地语上表现最差（79%）。超过70%的错误源于检索失败而非推理失败，且模型在面对包含虚假前提的问题时准确率从88-96%骤降至19-70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人正快速改变人们获取新闻的方式，但尚无研究系统性地衡量这些系统在处理跨语言、跨区域的即时事实时，其专有搜索集成和检索-合成管道的准确性如何。

Method: 在2026年2月9日至22日期间，基于BBC六个区域服务（美国&加拿大、阿拉伯语、非洲、印地语、俄语、土耳其语）的同日报道，构建2100个事实问题，对六款AI聊天机器人进行多项选择和自由回答两种形式的评估，并设计虚假前提问题测试模型脆弱性。

Result: 最佳系统多项选择准确率超90%，自由回答下降11-13%；印地语准确率最低（79%）；超过70%错误源于检索失败；面对虚假前提问题时，准确率从88-96%骤降至19-70%；检测准确率悖论：最佳虚假前提检测器在弃权率上仅排第二。

Conclusion: 高准确率可能掩盖系统性的区域不平等、对检索基础设施的近乎完全依赖，以及对真实用户可能提出的不完美查询的脆弱性。

Abstract: AI chatbots are rapidly shaping how people encounter the news, yet no prior study has systematically measured how accurately these systems, with their proprietary search integrations and retrieval-synthesis pipelines, handle emerging facts across languages and regions. We present a 14-day (February 9-22, 2026) evaluation of six AI chatbots (Gemini 3 Flash and Pro, Grok 4, Claude 4.5 Sonnet, GPT-5 and GPT-4o mini) on 2,100 factual questions derived from same-day BBC News reporting across six regional services (US & Canada, Arabic, Afrique, Hindi, Russian, Turkish). The best systems achieve over 90% multiple-choice accuracy on questions about events reported hours earlier. The same systems, however, lose 11-13% under free-response evaluation, and 16-17% across the cohort. We further characterize three failure patterns. First, every model achieves its lowest accuracy on Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) and citations indicate an Anglophone retrieval bias (e.g., models answering Hindi queries cite English Wikipedia more than any Hindi outlet). Second, retrieval, not reasoning, failures drive over 70% of all errors. When models retrieve a correct source, they often extract the correct answer; the problem is to land on the right source in the first place. Third, models achieving 88-96% accuracy on well-formed questions drop to 19-70% when questions contain subtle false premises, with the most vulnerable model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. We also identify a detection-accuracy paradox: the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy (abstention rate), while a weaker detector ranks first, showing that premise detection and answer recovery are partially independent capabilities. Overall, these suggest that high accuracy can mask systematic regional inequity, near-total dependence on retrieval infrastructure, and vulnerability to imperfect queries real users pose.

</details>


### [55] [Tokenisation via Convex Relaxations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22821)
*Jan Tempus,Philip Whittington,Craig W. Schmidt,Dennis Komm,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConvexTok，将分词器构建建模为线性规划问题并用凸优化工具求解，相比BPE和Unigram等贪心算法，在内在分词指标、语言模型BpB和下游任务上表现更优，且能证明其与最优解的差距在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分词算法（如BPE、Unigram）是贪心算法，仅做局部最优决策，未从全局角度考虑整个词汇表的质量，因此需要一种能全局优化分词器构建的方法。

Method: 将分词器构建形式化为线性规划问题，使用凸优化工具求解，得到新算法ConvexTok。

Result: ConvexTok持续改善了内在分词指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte（BpB），并在下游任务上也有提升（但一致性较弱）；实验表明在常见词汇表大小下，ConvexTok与最优解的差距在1%以内。

Conclusion: ConvexTok作为全局优化的分词构建方法，优于传统贪心算法，且能提供与最优解距离的认证保证。

Abstract: Tokenisation is an integral part of the current NLP pipeline. Current tokenisation algorithms such as BPE and Unigram are greedy algorithms -- they make locally optimal decisions without considering the resulting vocabulary as a whole. We instead formulate tokeniser construction as a linear program and solve it using convex optimisation tools, yielding a new algorithm we call ConvexTok. We find ConvexTok consistently improves intrinsic tokenisation metrics and the bits-per-byte (BpB) achieved by language models; it also improves downstream task performance, but less consistently. Furthermore, ConvexTok allows the user to certify how far their tokeniser is from optimal, with respect to a certain objective, via a lower bound, and we empirically find it to be within 1\% of optimal at common vocabulary sizes.

</details>

### [56] [Proximal Gradient-based Low Rank Tensor Decomposition for State Dependent Riccati Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21885)
*Jiahua Jiang,Carmeliza Navasca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于CP张量分解和稀疏优化的降阶方法，用于求解大规模离散控制系统的偏微分方程最优控制问题，通过低秩CP基元素构建降阶模型，并高效求解状态依赖Riccati方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决由偏微分方程衍生的大规模离散控制系统中最优控制问题的计算复杂性，传统方法难以处理高维控制空间的降阶需求。

Method: 利用快照数据构建张量数据集，通过CP（Canonical Polyadic）分解提取基元素；结合稀疏优化与灵活混合方法实现低秩CP张量基的降维；将降阶后的最优控制问题转化为低维状态依赖Riccati方程进行求解。

Result: 成功获得低秩CP张量基元素，构建了高效的降阶最优控制系统，降阶后的状态依赖Riccati方程可被高效求解。

Conclusion: 基于CP张量分解与稀疏优化的降阶方法能有效处理大规模离散控制系统的PDE最优控制问题，显著降低计算复杂度。

Abstract: We address the optimal control problems arising from partial differential equations with large discrete dimensional control systems. To obtain reduced order models, we find basis elements from the canonical polyadic (CP) decomposition. Tensor datasets are from snapshots of the large models. Our method to reduce the control system is to use dimensionality reduction approaches through sparse optimization and flexible hybrid methods is to obtain low rank CP tensor basis elements. The reduced optimal control problem leads to reduced state-dependent Riccati Equations which can be solved efficiently.

</details>


### [57] [Exact Uniform L1 Spacing for Solow-Polasky Diversity on Lines and Ordered Pareto Fronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21922)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich,Mahboubeh Nezhadmoghaddam,Jesús Guillermo Falcón Cardona*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究指数核下逆矩阵Solow-Polasky多样性的固定基数最大化问题，证明在[0,1]区间上最优k点子集为均匀间距点集，并将该结论推广到有序L1流形和双目标Pareto前沿的近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索有限度量空间上逆矩阵Solow-Polasky多样性（等价于指数核下的有限度量幅度）的固定基数最大化问题，寻找能在连续空间中产生均匀间距表示的最优子集选择准则。

Method: 基于有限线间隙公式（将过量逆矩阵多样性表示为连续间隙函数之和），通过区间定理证明均匀间距集是唯一最优解；通过逆命题证明相邻间隙加性结构迫使核函数为指数族；通过等距映射将结论推广到有序L1曲线。

Result: 对任意k≥2，[0,1]区间上唯一最大化k点子集是均匀间距集；反向命题证明在归一化非增距离核中，要求相邻间隙加性结构唯一确定指数核；该结论可等距转移到有序L1流形和单调双目标Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: Solow-Polasky多样性指标在连续空间中自然选择均匀间距表示，为多目标优化中Pareto前沿的均匀覆盖提供了理论依据和可计算的最优有限近似方案。

Abstract: We study fixed-cardinality maximization of the inverse-matrix Solow--Polasky diversity, equivalently finite metric magnitude for the exponential kernel, on one-dimensional and ordered metric sets. The analysis starts from the known finite-line gap formula for the exponential kernel, which writes the excess inverse-matrix diversity as a sum of functions of consecutive gaps. Building on this formula, the main interval theorem proves that, for every $k\geq 2$, the unique maximizing $k$-point subset of $[0,1]$ is the equally spaced set. Thus the objective selects a uniform gap representation on the real line. A converse kernel proposition shows that, among normalized non-increasing distance kernels, requiring the corresponding adjacent-gap additive structure forces the exponential family. Further results transfer the interval theorem to ordered $\ell_1$ (L1, or Manhattan) curves by isometry: the maximizing sets are uniform in accumulated $\ell_1$ length. As a consequence, monotone biobjective Pareto fronts admit Solow--Polasky optimal finite approximations that are uniformly spaced in accumulated objective-space change, a natural representation when all parts of a continuous front should be covered. Examples, including a dense connected front and a finite disconnected ZDT3 front, illustrate how the continuous uniform-gap result appears on discrete candidate sets.
  Solow-Polasky diversity; diversity measures; finite metric magnitude; L1 distance; uniform spacing; Pareto-front approximation; multiobjective optimization; fixed-cardinality subset selection

</details>


### [58] [Achieving Directional-Stationarity from a Single Random Direction Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22045)
*Dan Greenstein,Nadav Hallak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过在任意基础优化方法上添加随机方向探索步骤，即可在非光滑非凸约束优化中几乎必然保证方向平稳性（d-平稳性）的方法，且不破坏原方法的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束非光滑非凸优化中，标准方法通常只能保证弱平稳性（如Clarke平稳性），而方向平稳性（d-平稳性）作为更强的最优性条件难以获得。现有方法要么需要复杂结构假设，要么无法提供d-平稳性保证。

Method: 在任意基础优化方法（如DCA、prox-linear型方法）的迭代过程中，增加一个随机方向探索步骤：采样一个方向和步长，基于函数值比较决定是否接受候选点。该步骤可作为模块化组件加入。

Result: 所有聚点几乎必然达到d-平稳性，且该保证独立于基础优化方法的行为；同时保留基础方法的收敛速率（对DCA和prox-linear型方法已证明）。数值实验验证了该探索步骤的效果和保证。

Conclusion: 随机方向探索步骤是一种简单有效、可即插即用的方法，能在温和正则性条件（局部Lipschitz连续、方向导数存在且连续）下将任何优化方法提升至d-平稳性保证，且不牺牲收敛性能。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of obtaining strong optimality guarantees in constrained nonsmooth nonconvex optimization under mild regularity conditions, namely local Lipschitz continuity and existence and continuity of directional derivatives. While standard methods typically ensure weak stationarity notions, achieving directional (d-)stationarity remains nontrivial. We show that a random direction exploration step is sufficient to attain d-stationarity. The proposed approach augments any base optimization method with a single exploration step that samples a direction and step size and accepts the candidate based on a function value comparison. The resulting scheme guarantees that all accumulation points are d-stationary almost surely, independently of the behavior of the underlying method. Moreover, it preserves convergence rates of the base method, as established for DCA and prox-linear-type schemes. The theoretical results are complemented by numerical experiments illustrating the effect and guarantees of the exploration step.

</details>


### [59] [Online Optimization with Unknown Time-Varying Parameters from Noisy Gradient Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22251)
*Shivanshu Tripathi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对强凸成本函数中线性项受未知线性随机动态演化、且仅有噪声梯度观测的在线优化问题，提出结合Gauss-Markov参数重构、工具变量动态辨识与预测追踪的控制理论方法，并给出了期望追踪误差界与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际在线优化问题中，成本函数往往依赖不可测量且动态未知的时变潜变量，传统方法无法有效处理这种隐状态随未知随机动态演化的情形，因此需要新的方法来实现对潜变量的重构与对未来最优解的追踪。

Method: 1) 利用Gauss-Markov估计器从噪声梯度观测中重构潜变量参数；2) 采用工具变量估计器辨识参数动态；3) 基于辨识结果预测未来参数，进而计算未来最小化点。

Result: 给出了期望追踪误差的理论上界，并通过多组数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该控制理论驱动的框架能有效处理潜变量未知动态下的在线凸优化问题，在理论上保证了追踪性能，数值上展示了实用效果。

Abstract: We study online optimization problems in which the cost function depends on latent, time-varying parameters that are unmeasurable and governed by unknown dynamics. Specifically, we consider a strongly convex cost function whose linear term evolves according to unknown linear stochastic dynamics, while the algorithm has access only to finite noisy gradient measurements. We propose a solution that uses control theoretic tools to reconstruct the latent parameters from gradient observations using a Gauss-Markov estimator, then identifies the parameter dynamics using an instrumental-variable estimator, and finally forecasts the parameters to compute the future minimizer. We provide a bound on the expected tracking error. We illustrate the effectiveness of our algorithm on a series of numerical examples.

</details>


### [60] [Incentive-Aligned Vehicle-to-Vehicle Energy Trading via Nash-Integrated Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22363)
*Yujin Lin,Yue Yang,Hao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Nash-MADDPG方法，将纳什谈判解与多智能体深度确定性策略梯度相结合，用于V2V能量交易。该方法通过纳什谈判确定高效双边定价，并利用纳什引导的价格邻近奖励使智能体学习趋近于谈判最优策略。在30天连续运行评估中，社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，Jain公平指数提升40.1%，并在6-100智能体规模下验证了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2V能量交易可实现电动汽车间的去中心化点对点能量交换，降低电网依赖并变现富余容量。然而，协调具有不同充电需求和不确定到达-离开时间的自利型电动汽车智能体仍具挑战，现有方法要么需要集中优化（存在计算限制），要么缺乏公平性保证。

Method: 将纳什谈判解（Nash Bargaining Solution）集成到多智能体深度确定性策略梯度（MADDPG）中，提出Nash-MADDPG。纳什谈判确定高效双边定价，纳什引导的价格邻近奖励机制引导智能体学习趋近于谈判最优策略。

Result: 30天连续运行评估：社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，Jain公平指数提升40.1%。在6-100智能体规模下验证了可扩展性，定价稳定接近纳什谈判基准。

Conclusion: Nash-MADDPG有效实现了激励一致的去中心化V2V能量交易，在提升社会福利、交易量和公平性方面均优于双拍卖方法，且具有良好的可扩展性和定价稳定性。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) energy trading enables decentralized peer-to-peer energy exchange among electric vehicles (EVs), reducing grid dependency while monetizing surplus capacity. However, coordinating self-interested EV agents with diverse charging needs and uncertain arrival-departure schedules remains challenging. Existing approaches either require centralized optimization with computational limitations or lack fairness guarantees. This paper integrates Nash Bargaining Solution into Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient, namely Nash-MADDPG, for incentive-aligned V2V energy trading. Nash bargaining determines efficient bilateral pricing, while Nash-guided price proximity rewards align agent learning toward bargaining-optimal strategies. Evaluation over 30-day continuous operation demonstrates an improvement of 61.6% in social welfare and 62.9% improvement in trading volume over Double Auction, while achieving superior fairness, such as 40.1% improvement in Jain's index. Testing across 6-100 agents over a 30-day horizon with continuous vehicle turnover confirms scalability across population size and empirically stable pricing near the Nash Bargaining benchmark.

</details>

*Simone Pirrera,Francesco Ripa,Daniele Astolfi,Sophie M. Fosson,Vito Cerone,Diego Regruto*

Main category: math.OC

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于控制理论拉格朗日方法的等式约束优化动力学收敛结果依赖于目标函数的强凸性或有界性假设，缺乏对约束几何结构的利用。本文旨在通过利用约束流形的几何结构来放宽这些假设。

Method: 采用控制理论中的比例积分控制器和反馈线性化控制器诱导连续时间动力学，并利用约束流形的黎曼几何结构进行分析。

Result: 证明了在非凸问题中，若目标函数限制在约束流形上满足适当的凸性性质，则动力学系统具有全局指数收敛性。

Conclusion: 通过几何方法克服了传统强凸性假设的限制，将收敛性分析推广到更一般的非凸等式约束优化问题。

Abstract: This paper studies the flows of continuous-time dynamics for equality-constrained optimization based on control-theoretic Lagrangian methods. In particular, we consider dynamics induced by proportional-integral and feedback linearization controllers, which have been recently proposed as alternatives to primal-dual gradient methods. Unlike existing convergence results, which rely on strong convexity of the objective function or boundedness assumptions, we exploit the geometric structure induced by the constraints. Specifically, we show global exponential convergence for non-convex problems that satisfy a suitable convexity property when restricted to the constraint manifold.

</details>

*Xiaozhou Wang,Ting Kei Pong,Zev Woodstock*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合增广拉格朗日方法与条件梯度法的算法，用于求解带多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合凸优化问题，并证明了与已知最优复杂度相匹配的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理带多个光滑凸不等式约束且目标函数包含线性最小化预言机的复合凸优化问题时，缺乏高效的算法设计与收敛率分析，特别是当h为均匀凸集指示函数时加速率的缺失。

Method: 在每次AL迭代中，对AL函数执行一步条件梯度法（采用开环步长或短步长），然后按经典AL方法用递减对偶步长更新对偶变量。

Result: 在两种步长规则下均获得了与该类问题已知最优复杂度匹配的收敛率；当h为均匀凸集指示函数时，建立了加速收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提算法高效且具有理论保证，填补了该类问题在AL-CG混合框架下的收敛性分析空白。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing the sum of a Lipschitz differentiable convex function $f$ and a proper closed convex function $h$ that admits efficient linear minimization oracles, subject to multiple smooth convex inequality constraints. We adapt the classical augmented Lagrangian (AL) method for these problems: in each iteration, our algorithm consists of one step of the conditional gradient (CG) method applied to the AL function, followed by an update of the dual variable as in classical AL methods with a diminishing dual stepsize. We study the convergence rate of our algorithm under two standard stepsize rules for the CG method, namely, an open-loop stepsize and the short stepsize, and obtain a convergence rate that matches the best-known complexity for this class of problems. We also establish accelerated rates when $h$ is the indicator function of a uniformly convex set.

</details>

*Andrea Bisoffi,Wenjie Liu,Zhongjie Hu,Claudio De Persis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对MIMO离散时间线性系统，仅利用含未知外生信号噪声的输入输出数据，无需系统模型，通过半定规划设计反馈控制器，渐近消除外生信号对输出的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在系统模型未知且存在未知外生干扰噪声的情况下，如何仅从输入输出数据直接设计反馈控制器以实现输出调节，解决无模型环境下的输出调节问题。

Method: 在辅助系统上建立半定规划问题来设计控制器，并通过严格的数学分析建立辅助系统与原始系统之间解的关系，从而将控制器设计从辅助系统迁移到原始系统。

Result: 实现了仅依赖输入输出数据的MIMO线性系统输出调节控制器设计，控制器由半定规划求解得到。

Conclusion: 本文从数据驱动的角度解决了MIMO线性系统的输出调节问题，为无模型场景下的控制器设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: From a multi-input-multi-output (MIMO) discrete-time linear system, we collect input-output data affected by noise in the form of an unknown exosignal and, from these data points (without knowledge of the system model), we design a feedback controller that asymptotically annihilates the effect of that exosignal on the output. This amounts to solving an output regulation problem purely from input-output data, for MIMO linear systems. The design of the controller corresponds to a semidefinite program and is pursued on a suitable auxiliary system. Such design carries over from the auxiliary system to the original one by a rigorous examination of the relation between the solutions of the two systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称理论中同时包含玻色子和费米子变量的动力系统具有重要意义，但这类系统在控制理论中的研究尚不充分，因此需要将控制问题扩展到李超群这一更一般的配置空间上。

Method: 利用左不变向量场构成的李超子代数在全体光滑向量场构成的李超代数中的正规化子来刻画控制系统；在此基础上详细研究线性控制系统，并提出能控性判据。

Result: 给出了李超群上控制系统的正规化子刻画方法，建立了线性控制系统的能控性判据，并通过实例进行了验证。

Conclusion: 成功将控制理论扩展到包含超对称变量的李超群框架下，为超对称动力系统的控制问题提供了理论基础和能控性分析方法。

Abstract: The dynamical systems having both bosonic and fermionic variables play an important role in the theory of supersymmetry. This article addresses the control problems including both bosonic and fermionic variables on Lie supergroup as the configuration space. Here, the control systems are characterized using the normalizer of Lie subsuperalgebra of left-invariant vector fields in the Lie superalgebra of all smooth vector fields of Lie supergroup. Then, the linear control system is studied in detail and its controllability criterion is proposed along with suitable examples.

</details>


### [65] [Optimization over the intersection of manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22736)
*Yan Yang,Bin Gao,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了流形交集上的两种正则性（干净相交和内蕴横截性）等价，并提出了一种仅在一个流形上使用收缩的几何优化方法，沿两个正交方向更新迭代点，分别负责渐近接近另一个流形和降低目标函数。在稀疏与低秩优化问题上的实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个流形交集上的优化问题广泛存在，但可行区域的耦合几何特性使得优化困难，现有方法缺乏对正则性条件关系的理论认识及高效的几何优化策略。

Method: 1. 证明了干净相交与内蕴横截性两种正则性条件的等价性，从而得到可计算的切空间投影。2. 提出仅在一个流形上使用收缩的几何方法，沿两个正交方向更新：一个方向负责渐近接近另一个流形（可行性），另一个方向负责降低目标函数（最优性）。

Result: 在内蕴横截性条件下，推导了可行性和最优性指标的收敛率，证明每个聚点都是一阶稳定点。在球面数据拟合、双曲嵌入近似和压缩模式计算等稀疏与低秩优化问题上的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为流形交集优化提供了一种理论保证与实用性能兼备的几何方法，通过等价性证明简化了投影计算，双方向更新策略有效解耦了可行性与最优性，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Optimization over the intersection of two manifolds arises in a broad range of applications, but is hindered by the coupled geometry of the feasible region. In this paper, we prove that the regularities -- clean intersection and intrinsic transversality -- are equivalent, which yields a tractable projection onto the tangent space of the intersection. Therefore, we propose a geometric method that employs a retraction on only one manifold and updates the iterate along two orthogonal directions. Specifically, the iterates stay on one manifold, and the two directions are responsible for asymptotically approaching the other manifold and decreasing the objective function, respectively. Under intrinsic transversality, we derive the convergence rate for both the feasibility and optimality measures, and show that every accumulation point is first-order stationary. Numerical experiments on problems stemming from sparse and low-rank optimization, including fitting spherical data, approximating hyperbolic embeddings on real data, and computing compressed modes, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>

### [66] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了MOOD基准来评估LLM监控管道对分布外对齐失败的检测能力，发现守卫模型在OOD场景下泛化能力不足；通过结合守卫模型与OOD检测器（马氏距离和困惑度），可将召回率从39%提升至45%，且OOD检测带来的增益优于将守卫模型参数扩大20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全与对齐失败常源于分布外场景，现有监控管道对此类异常模式的检测能力尚未被系统评估，因此需要建立专门的基准来研究这一问题。

Method: 构建MOOD基准，包含受限训练集和七个分布外测试集；训练守卫模型（安全分类器）并结合四种OOD检测器（如马氏距离、困惑度）进行监控；在不同模型规模上评估组合方案的性能。

Result: 守卫模型在OOD场景下泛化能力差；结合马氏距离和困惑度OOD检测器可将召回率从39%提升至45%；OOD检测带来的召回率增益优于将守卫模型参数量扩大20倍。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为LLM监控的关键组成部分，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [67] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TO-Agents 是一个多智能体AI框架，将自然语言设计意图与迭代拓扑优化连接起来，通过视觉语言推理和评判机制自动优化设计，在60%的试验中生成符合偏好的设计，相比消融管道成功率提升6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计师通常需要手动将定性设计意图（如视觉风格、产品体验、可制造性）转化为与这些偏好无直接关联的求解器参数，这一过程繁琐且低效。

Method: 提出TO-Agents多智能体框架，将自然语言问题描述转换为验证后的求解器输入，运行拓扑优化求解器，渲染3D拓扑结构，利用多视角视觉-语言推理结合独立评判智能体对结果进行批评并修订求解器参数，最后通过制造智能体进行后处理以适配增材制造。

Result: 在悬臂梁基准和手机支架产品设计两个任务中，TO-Agents在60%的试验中生成至少一个符合审美偏好的设计，相比无视觉或历史反馈的消融管道成功率提升高达6倍。评判分数和人工评估表明该管道能识别有效参数杠杆、从不良修订中恢复并扩展设计探索。

Conclusion: 智能体拓扑优化可将设计师从低层参数调优转向更高层次的形式与功能规格说明，同时需要为可靠的自主工程设计建立安全机制。识别了包括过冲、选择性记忆、工具错位和参数推理错误等失败模式。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [68] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模计算分析（ discourse segmentation, topic modeling, LLM）比较了USC Shoah Foundation和Yale Fortunoff Video Archive的1,600+大屠杀幸存者证词，验证了二者结构化程度的差异，但也发现了显著重叠，挑战了简单的“结构化vs.自由形式”二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Holocaust研究中长期存在一种二元区分：USC Shoah Foundation的访谈采用结构化、采访者引导的形式，而Yale Fortunoff Video Archive则倾向于自由开放的形式。这一区分影响了学术研究和后续档案建设，但缺乏大规模、可量化的系统验证。本文旨在通过计算分析方法严格检验这一经典论断。

Method: 对超过1,600份证词进行话语分割、主题建模和大语言模型分析，通过主题连贯性、采访者-幸存者互动动态以及问题类型分布来量化证词的“结构化程度”。

Result: 总体证实了先前研究指出的结构差异（两个档案馆的证词在结构化程度上存在区别），但也发现了显著的跨馆重叠——无论是在单个访谈内部还是跨常见叙事模式层面。这一发现使得简单的“结构化vs.自由形式”二分法复杂化。

Conclusion: 本文不仅重新审视了Holocaust研究中的一个基础性论断，还提供了一个可扩展、可复现的比较语料库分析框架，展示了其在数字口述史、叙事分析以及公民科学标注平台设计中的更广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [69] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindLoom是一个通过组合式思维模式工程合成前沿推理数据的框架，它将推理问题难度视为原子知识-推理转换（思维模式）的累积，通过分解困难问题、训练检索模型、迭代组合思维模式生成新问题，并基于rollout评判标注难度和答案。在9个基准测试中，微调后的模型在多个STEM学科和数学推理任务上表现优于基线和外部数据方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理数据合成方法对控制问题难度的结构因素可见性有限，导致多样性狭窄和难度控制不稳定，需要一种能系统合成前沿级推理数据的方法。

Method: (1) 将困难问题解分解为思维模式链；(2) 训练检索模型将问题状态与兼容思维模式匹配；(3) 通过迭代应用检索到的思维模式组合新问题，并使用分布对齐采样；(4) 基于rollout的评判阶段标注难度并提供正确答案用于SFT。

Result: 在9个基准（涵盖5个STEM学科和4个数学推理任务）上，MindLoom微调模型优于基础模型、蒸馏和外部数据基线；消融实验验证了各组件贡献；分析表明MindLoom覆盖广泛推理模式并保持有效的难度控制。

Conclusion: MindLoom通过组合式思维模式工程有效合成高质量、高多样性的推理数据，为提升LLM推理能力提供了系统化的数据合成框架。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [70] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0，这是一个证据模型原型，旨在通过数据模型扩展来改进AOP-Wiki，以支持计算生成的AOPs和定量AOPs，增强FAIR原则和AI就绪性，并促进AOP框架与NAM的整合，服务于下一代风险评估及生物医学和One Health领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AOP-Wiki作为不良结局路径的全球知识库，其现有数据模型和基础设施已无法支持AOP的持续增长与演进，亟需现代化改造以应对监管科学、生物医学和One Health领域的新需求。

Method: 提出AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0证据模型原型，重点进行数据模型扩展、内部质量改进、证据结构化以增强FAIR性和AI就绪性，并改进AOP框架与NAM的整合。

Result: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0具体展示了数据模型扩展方案，以及如何利用AI和核心AOP原则来聚合和结构化AOP相关信息，为计算生成的AOPs和定量AOPs奠定基础。

Conclusion: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0有望推动AOP-Wiki转型，更好地服务于监管科学，并拓展AOP在生物医学和One Health领域的应用，同时支持下一代风险评估。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [71] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过探究AI对不合理类别成员（如"橄榄是交通工具吗？"）的归类判断，研究AI系统与人类在基本概念边界理解上的差异，发现AI在多个概念上与人类存在显著对齐偏差，且这种偏差会导致AI安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展具有类人常识理解能力的AI系统是实现安全可靠系统的关键步骤，而传统通过询问合理类别成员（如"汽车是交通工具吗？"）来探测概念理解的方法容易受到训练数据中模式记忆的影响，需要更有效的替代方案来准确评估AI的概念边界理解。

Method: 采用Rosch和Mervis经典心理学研究中的基础概念集，让AI系统对对象进行上级类别分配，同时包括正确类别分配和错误类别分配任务，并将结果与人类参与者的判断进行比较。

Result: AI系统在多个概念上与人类存在显著且有意义的差异，包括将"词语"归类为"交通工具"和"服装"，将某些"蔬菜"成员识别为"水果"，以及将非武器类别的示例分配给"武器"类别。

Conclusion: AI系统在概念边界理解上与人类存在系统性偏差，这些概念对齐错误会转化为有问题的下游行为，对AI安全性产生实际影响。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [72] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过逻辑推理任务实验发现：过度依赖AI会削弱个人技能发展，而适度使用且AI信息量高时可辅助学习；低信息量AI既无短期效益又损害长期能力，高信息量AI可提升短期表现但效果因人而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI融入人类问题解决过程时，对个人技能发展的具体影响机制，尤其是AI使用频率和信息量如何调节学习效果。

Method: 在受控的逻辑推理任务中，让参与者按需获取AI辅助，通过对比不同AI使用频率（重度/轻度/不使用）和信息量（高/低）条件下的任务表现和技能保持情况。

Result: 1）高频率使用AI与更弱的技能发展相关，重度使用者表现劣于同龄人；2）低信息量AI既不提升短期表现，移除后表现更差；3）高信息量AI提升短期表现，平均不损害长期表现，但存在异质性效应。

Conclusion: AI的作用具有双重性：在适当条件下可补充人类技能发展（增强独立推理），但在其他条件下会替代并削弱推理能力；需通过调节AI的可及性和使用方式来促进技能发展。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [73] [Latent-space Attacks for Refusal Evasion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21706)
*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新将拒绝抑制（refusal suppression）定义为对线性探测器的潜在空间逃逸攻击，将现有方法的消融操作解释为投影到决策边界的最小置信度逃逸攻击，并指出其局限性（停在边界），进而提出受控潜在空间逃逸攻击（CLE），通过将表示投影到边界之外的合规区域实现更优的攻击效果，在15个模型上取得了最先进的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抑制语言模型拒绝行为的方法（如消融拒绝方向）虽有效，但缺乏对潜在空间变换的 principled 解释，且未意识到其本质上是一种逃逸攻击，存在仅到达决策边界的局限性。

Method: 将拒绝抑制重新定义为对线性探测器的潜在空间逃逸攻击；指出 prior work 的差均值方向恰好定义了一个线性探测器，其消融操作等价于投影到决策边界（最小置信度逃逸）；提出受控潜在空间逃逸攻击（Controlled Latent-space Evasion），将表示投影到边界之外并用优化后的置信度控制逃逸程度。

Result: 在15个指令微调、多模态和推理模型上取得了最先进的攻击成功率，超越了现有的拒绝消融基线和专门的越狱攻击方法。

Conclusion: 拒绝抑制可被统一理解为潜在空间逃逸攻击，将表示推过决策边界进入合规区域能更有效地抑制安全对齐行为。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [74] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttuneBench是一个基于200场真实多人-模型对话的情感智能基准测试，评估模型在多轮对话中的情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断能力。研究发现这些能力彼此独立，且偏好对齐和响应质量比情绪标签准确性更具模型区分度，表明情感智能行为需要预测用户在特定上下文中期望的响应类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感智能基准依赖合成提示、单轮对话或第三方标注，无法直接测量模型在真实多轮对话中推断和响应参与者情绪状态的能力。

Method: 基于200场真实多人-模型对话，参与者与匿名化LLM进行多轮对话，并逐轮标注自己的情绪状态、模型行为及偏好响应。对11个模型评估其情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断能力。

Result: 模型在四个评估维度上的排名基本独立，表明情感智能行为可分解为独立的能力。偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情绪标签准确性更具模型区分度。

Conclusion: 情感智能行为需要预测特定用户在上下文中期望的响应类型，单轮或合成格式无法跨轮次捕捉这一区别。AttuneBench为评估这些能力及诊断模型特定优势和失败模式提供了框架。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [75] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现基于AI平台对话日志的职业暴露度测量存在系统性偏差：这些分数部分反映的是平台用户构成而非实际劳动力特征。不同平台输入导致ChatGPT后就业系数变化达1.9倍，同一供应商内消费者与企业渠道估计符号相反。按BLS劳动力份额重新加权后估计值衰减42-93%。理论分析表明非经典测量误差使替代效应低估程度大于增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛使用AI平台对话日志来测量职业暴露度，但该方法可能因平台用户基础与劳动力构成的差异而产生测量偏差。本研究旨在系统检验这种测量方法的有效性，量化偏差程度，并提供理论修正方案。

Method: 保持结果变量、样本、控制变量和估计方法固定，仅改变平台数据输入来源，比较ChatGPT后就业系数变化；对比同一供应商内消费者与企业渠道的估计差异；使用BLS劳动力份额进行重新加权；建立非经典测量误差的理论模型，推导概率极限和部分识别边界。

Result: 仅改变平台输入导致就业系数变化1.9倍；消费者与企业渠道估计符号相反；BLS加权后估计值衰减42-93%；非经典测量误差导致替代效应被低估的程度大于增强效应。

Conclusion: 基于AI平台对话日志的职业暴露度测量存在严重的测量误差，研究结论对平台选择高度敏感，需谨慎解释相关实证结果。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [76] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合因果关系与辩论推理的XAI方法，通过因果发现构建支持/反对特征交互的双极论证框架，并利用半稳定语义提取特征扩展来解释模型预测原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法虽能识别相关特征，但无法阐明模型做出特定决策的原因，缺乏因果层面的解释能力。

Method: 首先使用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系，然后将因果结构转换为双极论证框架以表示特征间的支持与反对关系，最后通过半稳定语义找到能够解释预测结果的特征扩展集。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上进行了实验验证，并与标准事后可解释性方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该方法通过因果与论证推理的结合，能够提供比传统方法更具因果深度的模型预测解释。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [77] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过综述70篇论文和调查106位专家，提出了AI谄媚行为的分类体系，区分了(1)针对用户立场/信念 vs. 个人特质/情感的谄媚，以及(2)显性直接 vs. 隐性间接的谄媚表现。研究发现现有工作集中于对用户信念的显性谄媚，而更隐性和针对人格的谄媚研究不足；同时专家对具体行为是否属于谄媚存在显著分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI谄媚缺乏统一定义，导致评估结果难以比较、缓解策略无法迁移、系统仍表现出其他形式的谄媚行为。需要建立统一的术语和分类体系。

Method: 综述70篇相关论文构建谄媚行为分类体系，并对106位专家进行问卷调查以评估领域共识。

Result: (1) 提出了二维分类体系：方向维度（信念vs.人格）和表达维度（显性vs.隐性）；(2) 发现现有研究偏重对用户信念的显性谄媚；(3) 94.3%专家认为谄媚是重大问题，但对具体行为认定存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: AI谄媚是一类广泛的行为家族，包含不同的测量挑战、干预需求和治理含义。所提出的分类体系为理解和应对这些行为提供了共享词汇。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research.
